## Numbers 1:1–3 (#1) (id: 175763)

**Who did Yahweh tell Moses to count in the census?**

Yahweh told Moses to count every man of Israel who was 20 years old or older and could go out to war.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 1:1-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Wilderness of Sinai (ID: `place:SinaiWilderness`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Egypt (ID: `place:Egypt`); Moses (ID: `person:Moses`); Aaron (ID: `person:Aaron`); Tent of Meeting (ID: `realia:TentOfMeeting`)

## Numbers 1:4 (#1) (id: en_tq_NUM_t0ct)

**Who would serve Moses as tribe leaders?**

A man from every tribe, the head of his ancestral family, would serve as his tribe’s leader.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 1:4

## Numbers 4:16 (#1) (id: 175827)

**Of what was Eleazar, the son of Aaron the priest, in charge?**

He was in charge of the oil for the light, the incense of spices, the regular grain offering, and the anointing oil. He was also in charge of the whole Dwelling and everything in it, including the holy place and its equipment.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 4:16

**What did Yahweh warn that the Kohathites must not go in to see, or they would die?**

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 4:20

**What was the work of the Gershonites?**

The work of the Gershonites was to carry the curtains of the Dwelling and the tent of meeting, its covering, the porpoise skin covering over it, and the curtain at the entrance of the tent of meeting. They were also to carry the hangings of the courtyard, the curtain at the entrance of the courtyard, their ropes, and all the equipment for their service.

He says that the person is unfaithful against Yahweh and is guilty.

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Be Unfaithful (ID: `keyterm:Unfaithful`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

**What did Yahweh tell Moses that a man or woman who sins against another person must do?**

That person must confess the sin, pay back the full cost of the guilt to the one who was wronged, and add one fifth more.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 5:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Repent (ID: `keyterm:Repent`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`)

**What must the guilty person do with the payment if the wronged person has no close relative to receive it?**

The guilty person must give the payment to Yahweh by giving it to the priest, along with a ram for atonement. The priest then owns it.

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Appease (ID: `keyterm:Appease`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Atonement (ID: `keyterm:Atonement`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`)

## Numbers 5:12 (#1) (id: en_tq_NUM_lxkc)

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Be Unfaithful (ID: `keyterm:Unfaithful`)

## Numbers 5:13 (#1) (id: en_tq_NUM_jkju)

**What did Yahweh say might be true of the wife even though her husband does not know about it?**

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 5:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`)

## Numbers 5:14 (#1) (id: en_tq_NUM_izy1)

A spirit of jealousy might come over him, and he would be jealous of his wife, whether she is unclean or not.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 5:14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Be Zealous (ID: `keyterm:Zealous`); Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`)

## Numbers 5:15 (#1) (id: en_tq_NUM_oj0n)

**What did Yahweh say a man must do with his wife if he is jealous and thinks she has sinned against him?**

The man must bring his wife to the priest with an offering of about two liters of barley flour. He must not put oil or frankincense on it, because it is a grain offering of jealousy that causes sin to be remembered.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 5:15

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Frankincense (ID: `flora:Frankincense`); Anointing Oil (ID: `realia:AnointingOil`); Olive Oil (ID: `realia:OliveOil`); Ephah (ID: `realia:Ephah`); Barley (ID: `flora:Barley`)

## Numbers 5:16–17 (#1) (id: 175844)

**What does the priest do first?**

The priest places the woman before Yahweh. Then he takes holy water in a clay jar and mixes dust from the floor of the Dwelling into the water.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 5:16-17

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Floor (ID: `realia:Floor`)

## Numbers 5:18–19 (#1) (id: en_tq_NUM_rrp5)

**Then what does the priest do?**

The priest lets the woman’s hair down and puts the grain offering of jealousy in her hands. While the priest holds the bitter water that brings a curse, he makes the woman swear an oath. He tells her that if she has not been unfaithful to her husband, the water will not harm her.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 5:18-19

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Curse (ID: `keyterm:Curse`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Be Blameless (ID: `keyterm:Blameless`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Swear (ID: `keyterm:Swear`); Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`)

The priest told her that Yahweh would make her a curse among her people by making her abdomen swell and her thigh waste away.

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Oath (ID: `keyterm:Oath`); Swear (ID: `keyterm:Swear`); Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Numbers 19:12 (#2) (id: 176112)

**What happened to anyone who touched a dead body and did not purify himself on the third day?**

The person who did not purify himself on the third day after touching a dead body would not be clean on the seventh day.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 19:12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Pure (ID: `keyterm:Pure`); To Cleanse (ID: `keyterm:Cleanse.2`)

## Numbers 19:13 (#1) (id: 176113)

**Why would the person who touched a dead body but did not purify himself, remain unclean; and what would happen to that man?**

The person who touched a dead body and did not purify himself would remain unclean because he had defiled Yahweh’s tabernacle. That man would be cut off from Israel, and he would remain unclean.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 19:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`); To Cleanse (ID: `keyterm:Cleanse.2`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Numbers 19:14 (#1) (id: 176114)

**What happened to any person who went into a tent where someone had died or who was already in the tent when someone died?**

Both those who went into the tent where someone had died and those who were already in the tent would be unclean for seven days.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 19:14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Instruction (ID: `keyterm:Instruction`); Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`); Tent (ID: `realia:Tent`)

## Numbers 19:15 (#1) (id: 176115)

**What happened to containers that were uncovered when someone died inside a tent?**

Every open container became unclean when someone died inside a tent.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 19:15

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`); Thread (ID: `realia:Thread`); Measuring Reed (ID: `realia:MeasuringReed`)

## Numbers 19:16 (#1) (id: 176116)

**What was the law for anyone outside a tent who touched someone who had been killed with a sword or who had touched another a dead body, a human bone, or a grave?**

The Israelite who touched someone who had been killed with a sword or who had touched a dead body, a human bone, or a grave would be unclean for seven days.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 19:16

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`)

## Numbers 19:17 (#1) (id: 176117)

**What had to be used for the unclean person, and in what was it mixed?**

Ashes from the burnt sin offering had to be used and were mixed in a jar with fresh water.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 19:17

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`)

## Numbers 19:18 (#1) (id: 176118)

**What did someone do with the ashes from the burnt sin offering that were mixed in a jar with fresh water?**

Using hyssop, someone who was clean had to sprinkle some of that water on the tent and on the things and people who were in the tent where someone had died. This water was also sprinkled on the person who had touched a human bone, a dead body, or a grave.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 19:18

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Pure (ID: `keyterm:Pure`); Hyssop (ID: `flora:Hyssop`); Tent (ID: `realia:Tent`)

## Numbers 19:21 (#1) (id: 176119)

**What happened to the one who sprinkled the water for impurity, and how long was he unclean?**

The one who sprinkled the water for impurity had to wash his clothes, and he was unclean until evening.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 19:21

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`)

## Numbers 19:22 (#1) (id: 176120)

**What happened to anyone who touched something that the unclean person had touched?**

Anyone who touched something that the unclean person had touched would be unclean until that evening.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 19:22

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`)

## Numbers 35:16–18 (#1) (id: 176399)

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 35:16-18


## Numbers 35:19 (#1) (id: en_tq_NUM_ka65)

The close relative of the dead person was responsible for killing the murderer whenever he found him.

## Numbers 35:20–21 (#1) (id: en_tq_NUM_s7vw)

**What would happen if an accused man pushed someone out of hatred, threw something at him on purpose, or struck him with his hand so that he died?**

## Numbers 35:22–23 (#1) (id: en_tq_NUM_xuvu)

Suddenly pushing someone, throwing something without meaning to hit them, or accidentally dropping a stone on someone without seeing them are examples of accidentally causing a person’s death.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 35:22-23


## Numbers 35:24 (#1) (id: en_tq_NUM_dmxa)

The community was responsible for judging between the accused person and the avenger, following these rules.

## Numbers 35:28 (#1) (id: 176403)
